{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"peoples","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"peeps","tags":["alternative","slang"]},{"form":"peops","tags":["alternative","slang"]},{"form":"peple","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]},{"form":"pipple","tags":["alternative","pronunciation-spelling"]},{"form":"ppl","tags":["alternative","Internet"]},{"form":"ppl.","tags":["alternative","Internet"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"There were so many people at the restaurant last night.","type":"example"},{"text":"These six people are waiting for the bus.","type":"example"},{"text":"Under dictatorship, people suffered and died.","type":"example"}],"form_of":[{"word":"person"}],"glosses":["plural of person: a body of persons considered generally or collectively; a group of two or more persons."],"synonyms":[{"tags":["slang"],"word":"peeps"},{"word":"lede"},{"word":"leod"}],"tags":["countable","form-of","plural","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"a people apart","type":"example"},{"text":"an industrious people","type":"example"},{"text":"the indigenous peoples of Europe","type":"example"},{"text":"the native peoples of Borneo","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Persons forming or belonging to a particular group, such as a nation, class, ethnic group, country, family, etc."],"synonyms":[{"word":"collective"},{"word":"community"},{"word":"congregation"},{"word":"folk"}],"tags":["collective","countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Moses said, \"Let my people go.\"","type":"example"},{"text":"his people were weary","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A group of persons regarded as being servants, followers, companions or subjects of a ruler or leader."],"synonyms":[{"word":"fans"},{"word":"groupies"},{"word":"supporters"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I'll have my people call your people.","type":"example"},{"text":"I have my people working on it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One's colleagues or employees."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"My people lived through the Black Plague and the Thirty Years War.","type":"example"},{"text":"His people live out west.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A person's ancestors, relatives or family."],"synonyms":[{"word":"kin"},{"word":"kith"},{"word":"folks"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The people have successfully stood up against tyranny and totalitarianism.","type":"example"},{"text":"The election is over and the people have spoken.","type":"example"},{"text":"The people won't tolerate so much corruption any more!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The mass of a community as distinguished from a special class (elite);
